# MASCI Operational Execution Constitutional Appendix

## 1. Appendix Authority

This appendix is a mandatory part of the MASCI Operational Execution constitutional set. It supplies normative catalogs required to eliminate governance ambiguity and is read together with the Requirement Traceability Matrix.

## 2. Stable Identifier Catalog

### 2.1 Identifier Rules
- Display names, labels, and free text are never canonical cross-domain identifiers.
- Email is never lifetime professional identity.
- Fuzzy text matching may assist discovery only and may never replace stable identifier linkage.
- Labels/codes may change without changing identity.
- Historical snapshots retain the labels/codes that existed at the time of record without severing stable identifiers.
- Identifiers are never reused after deletion, retirement, cancellation, or supersession.

| Identifier Requirement ID | Canonical Field Name | Governed Concept | Canonical Data Type | Owner/Issuer | Generation Method | Uniqueness Scope | Tenant Scope | Project Scope | Immutability Rule | Human-Readable or Internal | External Exposure Rule | Label/Code Relationship | Snapshot Behavior | Revision/Version Relationship | Migration Behavior | Legacy Compatibility | Reuse Prohibition | Deletion Behavior | Split Behavior | Merge Behavior | Cross-Domain Linkage Rule | Search/Display Rule | Audit Requirement | Certification Test |

## 3. Lifecycle Catalog

### 3.1 Lifecycle Rules
- Every state machine is closed-set.
- Every state and transition is version-aware, auditable, event-emitting, and traceable to Trust where operational lifecycle proof is required.
- Terminal-state protection is mandatory.
- Split, merge, revision, supersession, cancellation, and reopen actions preserve lineage and history.

### 3.2 State Catalogs

| RC-004 | SAFETY_HOLD | Safety condition blocks or constrains work |
| RC-005 | QA_HOLD | Quality condition blocks or constrains work |
| RC-006 | RESOURCE_UNAVAILABLE | Required crew/equipment/material/trucking unavailable |
| RC-007 | SCOPE_SPLIT | Scope divided into governed child identities |
| RC-008 | SCOPE_MERGE | Scope consolidated into governed successor identity |
| RC-009 | CORRECTION | Governed correction or amendment |
| RC-010 | CANCELLATION | Authorized cancellation with preserved history |

## 4. Canonical Event Envelope and Delivery Semantics

### 4.1 Event Envelope Field Specification

| Event Requirement ID | Field | Requirement Status | Type | Validation | Allowed Null Behavior | Canonical Source | Sensitive-Data Restrictions | Size / Compatibility Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EVT-001 | event_id | required | string | must be unique opaque durable id | not null | event issuer | never include PII semantics | version-safe opaque id |
| EVT-002 | event_type | required | string | must be from canonical vocabulary | not null | domain emitter | no narrative leakage | backward-compatible additive only |
| EVT-003 | event_version | required | string/int | supported version only | not null | domain emitter | no sensitive content | unsupported version must fail safely |
| EVT-004 | tenant_id | required | string | must match source tenant | not null | source record | tenant isolation enforced | cross-tenant rejected |
| EVT-005 | project_id | conditionally required | string | required when project-scoped | null only for non-project concepts | source record | permission-safe only | project-scope consumers validate |
| EVT-006 | entity_type | required | string | must match governed concept vocabulary | not null | emitter | no sensitive narrative | strict enum evolution |
| EVT-007 | entity_id | required | string | must map to stable id catalog | not null | source record | no raw secret material | stable id only |
| EVT-008 | source_family | required | string | must map to source family vocabulary | not null | source authority | no sensitive narrative | consumer-compatible |
| EVT-009 | source_type | required | string | must map to source type vocabulary | not null | source authority | no sensitive narrative | consumer-compatible |
| EVT-010 | source_version | required | string/int | must identify source schema/version | not null | source authority | no sensitive narrative | backward-compatible handling |
| EVT-011 | source_record_id | required | string | must reference canonical source record | not null | source record | permission-safe only | stable source linkage |
| EVT-012 | source_revision | conditionally required | string/int | required where revised source exists | null only if source not revisioned | source record | no sensitive narrative | consumer handles absent revision safely |
| EVT-013 | actor_id | required | string | must identify human/system/AI actor | not null | execution context | PII policy applies | stable linkage to actor identity |
| EVT-014 | actor_role | required | string | must match governed role vocabulary | not null | execution context | no sensitive narrative | strict role vocabulary |
| EVT-015 | occurred_at | required | timestamp | must be valid timestamp | not null | event occurrence | no restriction | timezone-safe |
| EVT-016 | recorded_at | required | timestamp | must be valid timestamp | not null | event persistence layer | no restriction | must not predate occurred_at materially |
| EVT-017 | correlation_id | conditionally required | string | required for multi-step workflows | null only for isolated non-chained events | workflow chain | no sensitive narrative | stable chain linkage |
| EVT-018 | causation_id | conditionally required | string | required when event caused by prior event | null only when no prior event exists | workflow chain | no sensitive narrative | stable causality linkage |
| EVT-019 | before | optional | object | must be structured and redacted | null allowed when no prior state snapshot needed | source transition | redact sensitive fields | size-bounded diff snapshot |
| EVT-020 | after | optional | object | must be structured and redacted | null allowed where event has no resulting state body | source transition | redact sensitive fields | size-bounded diff snapshot |
| EVT-021 | reason_code | conditionally required | string | required for non-routine/corrective state change | null only for routine non-reasoned events | workflow rule | no sensitive narrative | closed-set reason vocabulary |
| EVT-022 | evidence_refs | conditionally required | array | required where evidence-gated event exists | empty only when no evidence required | source workflow | permission-safe evidence ids only | reference ids only, no raw storage keys |
| EVT-023 | status | required | string | must use governed status vocabulary for event processing | not null | event delivery/state machine | no sensitive narrative | consumer-compatible enum |
| EVT-024 | metadata | optional | object | must remain governed and non-authoritative | null allowed | emitter | no broad sensitive dump | size-limited, schema-evolvable |
| EVT-025 | idempotency_key | conditionally required | string | required when retry/replay possible | null only when replay impossible by contract | delivery layer | no sensitive narrative | duplicate detection safe |
| EVT-026 | sensitivity_classification | required | string | must classify sensitivity | not null | security contract | controls downstream visibility | strict enum |
| EVT-027 | redaction_policy | required | string | must identify applied redaction rule | not null | security contract | enforces privacy | consumer-compatible |
| EVT-028 | schema_version | required | string/int | must identify envelope schema version | not null | event contract | no sensitive narrative | strict compatibility handling |

### 4.2 Event Delivery Semantics

- Delivery expectation: effectively-once business outcome over at-least-once transport through idempotency keys and duplicate detection.
- Duplicate detection: consumers must reject duplicate business effects while retaining audit of duplicate delivery attempts.
- Replay handling: replay is allowed only if idempotent or explicitly rebuild-oriented; replay may not mutate canonical source truth directly.
- Ordering guarantee: per-entity best-effort ordering where available; no global ordering guarantee.
- Out-of-order events: consumers must reconcile against source truth, correlation/causation, and source revision before applying derived effects.
- Late events: consumers may update derived projections, notifications, briefs, search, or caches only through governed stale/revision behavior.
- Eventual consistency: derived consumers may lag, but lag must be visible where operator-visible truth depends on freshness.
- Consumer retry: governed retry with idempotency and poison-event handling required.
- Dead-letter / poison handling: failed or poison events must surface failed/retry status and remain recoverable without corrupting canonical state.
- Failed serialization, persistence, or delivery: no false success; emit failure telemetry/audit and preserve retry or manual recovery path.
- Unknown event type / unsupported version / unknown entity / missing source record / orphan event / cross-tenant event: reject or quarantine by governed security and compatibility rules; do not create silent side effects.

| Dashboard Requirement ID | Surface | Role Mission | Primary Users | Tenant/Project Scope | Allowed Source Classes | Prohibited Source Classes | Data Coverage | Freshness / Current-Through | Critical Exceptions | Operational Board | Action Queue | Resource / Readiness View | Recent Changes | Evidence Drill-Down | History / Trends | Search / Filters | Mobile / iPad / Desktop | English / Spanish | Empty State | Permission Behavior | Certification |

## 6. Notification Contract

### 6.1 Notification Field Contract

| Notification Requirement ID | Field | Binding Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| NOTIF-001 | notification_type_id | Required, stable notification-type identifier |
| NOTIF-002 | trigger | Required source condition that makes the notification eligible |
| NOTIF-003 | source_event | Required canonical event linkage |
| NOTIF-004 | evidence | Required source evidence reference |
| NOTIF-005 | tenant/project scope | Required scope resolution |
| NOTIF-006 | audience | Required recipient class |
| NOTIF-007 | recipient-resolution owner | Required owner of recipient resolution |
| NOTIF-008 | priority | Required priority from INFORMATIONAL/NORMAL/IMPORTANT/URGENT/CRITICAL |
| NOTIF-009 | required_action | Required action or explicit informational purpose |
| NOTIF-010 | action_owner | Required owner of the action |
| NOTIF-011 | channel | Required bell/email/digest/task/alarm channel mapping |
| NOTIF-012 | timing | Required timing behavior |
| NOTIF-013 | expiration | Required expiration behavior |
| NOTIF-014 | dedupe key | Required duplicate-prevention key |
| NOTIF-015 | suppression rule | Required suppression behavior |
| NOTIF-016 | escalation rule | Required escalation behavior |
| NOTIF-017 | retry rule | Required retry behavior |
| NOTIF-018 | delivery status | Required lifecycle visibility |
| NOTIF-019 | resolution condition | Required resolution rule |
| NOTIF-020 | lifecycle state | Required notification lifecycle state |
| NOTIF-021 | deep link | Required permission-safe deep link |
| NOTIF-022 | permission behavior | Required scope enforcement |
| NOTIF-023 | English text | Required English user-facing text |
| NOTIF-024 | Spanish text | Required Spanish user-facing text |
| NOTIF-025 | audit event | Required audit linkage |
| NOTIF-026 | Trust relationship | Required Trust relationship where applicable |
| NOTIF-027 | certification/test suppression | Required suppression during certification/testing |
| NOTIF-028 | synthetic-record suppression | Required synthetic-record suppression |

### 6.3 Ecosystem Reuse Rule
- The existing MASCI notification ecosystem is mandatory.
- Duplicate notification engines are prohibited.
- Routine-success spam is prohibited.
- AI may not invent urgency without evidence.

## 7. Daily Company Operations Brief Contract

### 7.1 Brief Identity and Publication Fields

| Brief Requirement ID | Field / Rule | Binding Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| BRIEF-001 | brief_id | Required stable brief identifier |
| BRIEF-002 | tenant_id | Required tenant boundary |
| BRIEF-003 | reporting_date | Required report date |
| BRIEF-004 | reporting_window_start | Required reporting window start |
| BRIEF-005 | reporting_window_end | Required reporting window end |
| BRIEF-006 | timezone | Required company timezone |
| BRIEF-007 | version_number | Required version number |
| BRIEF-008 | revision_number | Required revision number |
| BRIEF-009 | revision_reason_code | Required governed reason for revision when revised |
| BRIEF-010 | supersedes_brief_id | Required when superseding prior brief version |
| BRIEF-011 | lifecycle_state | Required lifecycle state |
| BRIEF-012 | generated_at | Required generation timestamp |
| BRIEF-013 | reviewed_at | Required when review occurs |
| BRIEF-014 | published_at | Required when publication occurs |
| BRIEF-015 | finalized_at | Required when final-close occurs |
| BRIEF-016 | data_through | Required current-through timestamp |
| BRIEF-017 | source_coverage_snapshot_id / source_schema_version / generator_version / generated_by / reviewed_by / approved_by / delivery_batch_id / certification_record / synthetic_record / real_user_delivery_suppressed | Required governed source, actor, delivery, and certification suppression fields |

### 7.2 Fixed Section Order
Brief Identity → Data Coverage → Executive Summary → Company Operations Snapshot → What Went Well → What Did Not Go as Planned → Project Highlights → Schedule and Reconciliation → Safety → Dispatch / Transportation / Trucking → Shop / Fleet / Equipment → Materials → QA/QC → Workforce / Qualifications → Constraints and Blockers → Executive Attention Required → Today’s Risks and Required Decisions → Evidence / Drill-Down Appendix

### 7.3 Revision Numbering and Material-Change Rules
- Preliminary initial generation must be explicitly labeled PRELIMINARY.
- Published version must carry immutable version_number and revision_number.
- Material late-data revision must create a new revision number and preserve prior version lineage.
- Final version must remain historically available and may only be superseded by governed successor.
- Minor non-material regeneration must not masquerade as changed operational truth.
- Silent replacement and version-number reuse are prohibited.

### 7.5 Delivery Behavior
- In-app behavior: full canonical brief, evidence drill-down, role/permission aware, version history, current-through timestamps, source warnings, and revision comparison.
- Email behavior: concise executive delivery, canonical version reference, preserved key warnings, deep link to full brief, delivery status, retry, and no duplicate delivery.
- PDF behavior: immutable version identity, generation timestamp, reporting window, data-through timestamp, source coverage, warnings, fixed section order, evidence appendix, archive and reproducibility.
- Delivery failure rules: provider rejection, partial recipient failure, retry, dedupe, escalation, in-app availability despite email failure, audit/Trust event, admin visibility, and no false DELIVERED state.
- Certification suppression rules: synthetic/certification briefs do not notify real users; certification recipients are controlled; operational KPIs exclude synthetic briefs; exports/search/history classify certification artifacts.

## 8. Security / Tenant Contract

The Security / Tenant Contract is implemented per foundational object in the Zero-Drift Matrix Security Boundary Matrix. Shared rules apply to every row and may never be used to omit object-level fields.

| Security Requirement ID | Shared Rule |
|---|---|
| SEC-001 | tenant_id is the root isolation boundary. |
| SEC-002 | company membership determines governed human access eligibility. |
| SEC-003 | cross-tenant access is prohibited unless an explicit governed support/admin override rule permits scoped inspection. |
| SEC-004 | project scope never widens beyond source authority. |
| SEC-005 | object-level authorization is mandatory. |
| SEC-006 | field-level restrictions apply where PII, HR, safety, evidence, executive, or privileged content exists. |
| SEC-007 | internal/public/private classification must be explicit. |
| SEC-008 | sensitive-data classification must be explicit. |
| SEC-009 | attachments and evidence inherit parent visibility; raw storage-key leakage is prohibited. |
| SEC-010 | search visibility may never exceed source visibility. |
| SEC-011 | dashboard visibility may never become source-truth mutation authority. |
| SEC-012 | brief visibility is governed and role-scoped. |
| SEC-013 | notification recipient scope is governed and permission-safe. |
| SEC-014 | export and PDF permissions are explicit. |
| SEC-015 | AI input/output privacy and redaction are explicit. |
| SEC-016 | event and audit redaction policies are explicit. |
| SEC-017 | system automation authority is deterministic only. |
| SEC-018 | synthetic/certification isolation is mandatory. |
| SEC-019 | unauthorized count/metadata leakage is prohibited. |
| SEC-020 | negative authorization tests are mandatory for every foundational object. |

## 9. MASCI Product Identity and Experience Contract

| UX Requirement ID | Binding Rule |
|---|---|
| UX-001 | Reuse shared MASCI shell / PortalShell or canonical shared shell. |
| UX-002 | Reuse shared navigation and page-header hierarchy. |
| UX-003 | Reuse shared typography, spacing, cards, panels, statuses, selectors, drawers/modals, evidence timeline, drill-down, empty states, responsive behavior, and permission-aware navigation. |
| UX-004 | Every new surface must look like, sound like, and behave like MASCI OPS. |
| UX-005 | No one-off themes, generic scheduling-product visual language, spreadsheet-first drift, or portal identity drift. |
| UX-010 | English remains canonical backend language. |
| UX-011 | All user-facing labels, validation, coaching, notifications, status labels, and brief headings must support English and Spanish. |
| UX-012 | No new English-only workflow is permitted. |
| UX-013 | Missing translations block applicable certification. |
| UX-020 | No color-only state communication. |
| UX-021 | Screen-reader labels, keyboard navigation, focus states, sufficient touch targets, semantic headings, accessible tables or mobile alternatives, accessible error summaries, and accessible evidence drill-down are required where applicable. |
| UX-022 | No clipped controls or horizontal scrolling on phone/iPad; progressive disclosure and plain heavy-civil language required. |

## 10. Manual GitHub / Deployment Boundary Contract

| Deployment Requirement ID | Binding Rule |
|---|---|
| DEPLOY-001 | ONLY JAYMN MAY PHYSICALLY SAVE OR PUBLISH CHANGES TO GITHUB. |
| DEPLOY-002 | ONLY JAYMN MAY PHYSICALLY DEPLOY PREVIEW OR PRODUCTION. |
| DEPLOY-003 | Emergent must never be instructed to push, publish, save to GitHub, deploy preview, deploy production, or claim those manual actions occurred. |
| DEPLOY-004 | Emergent may inspect, implement locally, run tests, create evidence, prepare handoff, and verify later stages only after Jaymn confirms the relevant manual action occurred. |

### 10.2 Workflow Milestones
GOVERNANCE_DOCUMENTED, LOCAL_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPLETE, LOCAL_TESTS_VERIFIED, READY_FOR_JAYMN_GITHUB_SAVE, JAYMN_GITHUB_SAVE_CONFIRMED, GITHUB_SOURCE_VERIFIED, READY_FOR_JAYMN_PREVIEW_DEPLOYMENT, JAYMN_PREVIEW_DEPLOYMENT_CONFIRMED, PREVIEW_VERIFIED, READY_FOR_JAYMN_PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT, JAYMN_PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT_CONFIRMED, PRODUCTION_VERIFIED, FIELD_ACCEPTANCE_VERIFIED, EXECUTIVE_ACCEPTANCE_VERIFIED, FINAL_OPERATIONAL_CLOSEOUT
